        function setLanguage(lang) {
            currentLang = lang;
            const t = translations[lang];

            document.getElementById('langBtnBn').className = lang === 'bn' ? 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white transition' : 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-slate-200 hover:text-white transition';
            document.getElementById('langBtnEn').className = lang === 'en' ? 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white transition' : 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-slate-200 hover:text-white transition';
            const nBn = document.getElementById('noticeLangBtnBn');
            const nEn = document.getElementById('noticeLangBtnEn');
            if (nBn && nEn) {
                nBn.className = lang === 'bn' ? 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white transition' : 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-slate-200 hover:text-white transition';
                nEn.className = lang === 'en' ? 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white transition' : 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-slate-200 hover:text-white transition';
            }
            const nLogin = document.getElementById('noticeLoginBtnText');
            if (nLogin) nLogin.innerText = t.dashLogin;

            document.getElementById('brandTitleText').innerText = t.brand;
            document.getElementById('dashLoginBtnText').innerText = t.dashLogin;
            document.getElementById('heroBadgeText').innerText = t.badge;
            document.getElementById('heroTitleText').innerText = t.title;
            document.getElementById('heroSubText').innerText = t.sub;
            document.getElementById('feat1Text').innerText = t.feat1;
            document.getElementById('feat2Text').innerText = t.feat2;
            document.getElementById('feat3Text').innerText = t.feat3;
            document.getElementById('bannerFooterText').innerHTML = `✨ <span class="text-blue-300 font-bold">${lang === 'bn' ? 'সেরা অঙ্গীকার:' : 'Commitment:'}</span> ${t.bannerFooter.replace('✨ ', '')}`;

            document.getElementById('jobSecTitle').innerText = t.jobSecTitle;

            const nbTitle = document.getElementById('noticeBoardHeadTitle');

            document.getElementById('modalApplyMainTitle').innerText = t.modalApplyMainTitle;

            document.getElementById('lblAppMobile').innerText = t.lblAppMobile;
            document.getElementById('lblAppEmail').innerText = t.lblAppEmail;
            document.getElementById('lblAppCv').innerText = t.lblAppCv;
            document.getElementById('lblAppCvSub').innerText = t.lblAppCvSub;
            document.getElementById('lblAppSubmitBtn').innerText = t.lblAppSubmitBtn;

        window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', (event) => {
            const selectBox = document.getElementById('teamSelect');
            
            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(function(teams) {
                    if (teams && teams.length > 0) {
                        selectBox.innerHTML = '<option value="" id="optTeamDefault">-- টিম নির্বাচন করুন --</option>';
                        teams
                          .filter(function(team) {
                              return String(team || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== 'anonymous complaint';
                          })
                          .forEach(function(team) {
                              const opt = document.createElement('option');
                              opt.value = team;
                              opt.textContent = team;
                              selectBox.appendChild(opt);
                          });
                    }
                })
                .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                    console.error("Team load error: ", err.message);
                })
                .getTeamList();

            const savedEmail = localStorage.getItem('carrybee_dash_email');
            if (savedEmail) {
                document.getElementById('dashEmailInput').value = savedEmail;

                document.getElementById('topHeaderBlock').classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('tabNavContainer').classList.add('hidden');
                if (document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab')) document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab').classList.add('hidden');
                if (document.getElementById('btnTrackTab')) document.getElementById('btnTrackTab').classList.add('hidden');
                switchTab('dashboardTab');

                setTimeout(function() {
                    loadDashboardData();
                }, 150);
            }
        });

        // USER LOGIN FIX: Exit Job/Notice full view before opening Dashboard Login
        function handleUserLoggingClick() {
            if (window.JOB_exitFullView) window.JOB_exitFullView();
            if (window.NBOARD_exitFullView) window.NBOARD_exitFullView();

            const topHeader = document.getElementById('topHeaderBlock');
            const tabNav = document.getElementById('tabNavContainer');
            if (topHeader) topHeader.classList.remove('hidden');
            if (tabNav) tabNav.classList.remove('hidden');

            const btnSubmit = document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab');
            const btnTrack = document.getElementById('btnTrackTab');
            if (btnSubmit) btnSubmit.classList.remove('hidden');
            if (btnTrack) btnTrack.classList.remove('hidden');

            switchTab('dashboardTab');
            const dashCard = document.getElementById('dashLoginCard');
            const dashPanel = document.getElementById('dashMainPanel');
            if (dashCard) dashCard.classList.remove('hidden');
            if (dashPanel && !dashboardLoggedEmail) dashPanel.classList.add('hidden');
            window.scrollTo({top: 0, behavior: 'smooth'});
        }

        function copyToClipboard(elementIdOrText, isTextDirect = false) {
            const sourceEl = !isTextDirect ? document.getElementById(elementIdOrText) : null;
            const textToCopy = String(isTextDirect ? elementIdOrText : (sourceEl ? sourceEl.innerText : '')).trim();
            if (!textToCopy) return;

            const copiedToast = currentLang === 'bn'
                ? '📋 Tracking Number কপি হয়েছে: ' + textToCopy
                : '📋 Tracking Number copied: ' + textToCopy;

            const fallbackCopy = () => {
                const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
                ta.value = textToCopy;
                ta.setAttribute('readonly', '');
                ta.style.position = 'fixed';
                ta.style.opacity = '0';
                document.body.appendChild(ta);
                ta.select();
                try { document.execCommand('copy'); showDashboardToast(copiedToast, 'success'); }
                catch (err) { showDashboardToast(currentLang === 'bn' ? 'কপি করা যায়নি' : 'Copy failed', 'error'); }
                document.body.removeChild(ta);
            };

            if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
                navigator.clipboard.writeText(textToCopy)
                    .then(() => showDashboardToast(copiedToast, 'success'))
                    .catch(() => fallbackCopy());
            } else {
                fallbackCopy();
            }
        }

        function switchTab(tab) {
            const btnSubmit = document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab');
            const btnTrack = document.getElementById('btnTrackTab');
            
            const submitTab = document.getElementById('submitTab');
            const trackTab = document.getElementById('trackTab');
            const dashboardTab = document.getElementById('dashboardTab');

            if(btnSubmit) btnSubmit.classList.remove('active');
            if(btnTrack) btnTrack.classList.remove('active');
            
            submitTab.classList.add('hidden');
            trackTab.classList.add('hidden');
            dashboardTab.classList.add('hidden');

            if (tab === 'submitTab') {
                if(btnSubmit) btnSubmit.classList.add('active');
                submitTab.classList.remove('hidden');
            } else if (tab === 'trackTab') {
                if(btnTrack) btnTrack.classList.add('active');
                trackTab.classList.remove('hidden');
            } else if (tab === 'dashboardTab') {
                dashboardTab.classList.remove('hidden');
            }

            document.body.classList.toggle('dashboard-mode', tab === 'dashboardTab');
        }

        function toggleOtherInput(isOther) {
            const box = document.getElementById('otherCategoryBox');
            const input = document.getElementById('otherCategoryText');
            if (isOther) {
                box.classList.remove('hidden');
                input.required = true;
            } else {
                box.classList.add('hidden');
                input.required = false;
            }
        }

        function handleFileSelect(event) {
            uploadedFiles = Array.from(event.target.files);
            renderFilePreviews();
        }

        function renderFilePreviews() {
            const container = document.getElementById('filePreviewContainer');
            const list = document.getElementById('fileList');
            list.innerHTML = '';

            if (uploadedFiles.length === 0) {
                container.classList.add('hidden');
                return;
            }

            container.classList.remove('hidden');

            uploadedFiles.forEach((file, index) => {
                const sizeMB = (file.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2);
                const fileItem = document.createElement('div');
                fileItem.className = 'flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm';
                
                fileItem.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-file text-cb-yellowDark text-lg"></i>
                        <span class="font-bold text-slate-700 truncate max-w-[180px] sm:max-w-[280px]">${file.name}</span>
                        <span class="text-slate-400 text-xs">(${sizeMB} MB)</span>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" onclick="removeFile(${index})" class="text-red-500 hover:text-red-700 p-1">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-xmark text-xl"></i>
                    </button>
                `;
                list.appendChild(fileItem);
            });
        }

        function removeFile(index) {
            uploadedFiles.splice(index, 1);
            renderFilePreviews();
        }

        function convertBase64(file) {
            return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const fileReader = new FileReader();
                fileReader.readAsDataURL(file);
                fileReader.onload = () => resolve(fileReader.result);
                fileReader.onerror = (error) => reject(error);
            });
        }

        function openConfirmationModal(event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            document.getElementById('confirmationModal').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function closeConfirmationModal() {
            document.getElementById('confirmationModal').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        async function confirmAndFinalSubmit() {
            const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('confirmSubmitBtn');
            confirmBtn.disabled = true;
            confirmBtn.innerText = currentLang === 'bn' ? "জমা হচ্ছে..." : "Submitting...";

            let selectedCat = document.querySelector('input[name="category"]:checked')?.value || 'অন্যান্য';
            if (selectedCat === 'অন্যান্য (Other)') {
                selectedCat = 'অন্যান্য: ' + (document.getElementById('otherCategoryText').value || '');
            }

            let fileData = null;
            if (uploadedFiles.length > 0) {
                const file = uploadedFiles[0];
                const base64 = await convertBase64(file);
                fileData = {
                    name: file.name,
                    type: file.type,
                    data: base64.split(",")[1]
                };
            }

            const record = {
                name: document.getElementById('userName').value.trim(),
                mobile: document.getElementById('userPhone').value.trim(),
                team: document.getElementById('teamSelect').value,
                location: document.getElementById('personInvolved').value.trim() || 'N/A',
                category: selectedCat,
                problem: document.getElementById('complaintDetails').value.trim(),
                confirmed: true,
                file: fileData
            };

            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
                    confirmBtn.disabled = false;
                    confirmBtn.innerText = currentLang === 'bn' ? "✓ আমি সম্মত" : "✓ I Agree";

                    if (result && result.success) {
                        document.getElementById('generatedId').innerText = result.complaintId;
                        const emailNotice = document.getElementById('emailSendNotice');
                        if (emailNotice) {
                            if (result.emailSent) {
                                emailNotice.className = 'text-sm font-bold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3';
                                emailNotice.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check mr-1"></i> Notification email পাঠানো হয়েছে।';
                            } else {
                                emailNotice.className = 'text-sm font-bold text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3';
                                emailNotice.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation mr-1"></i> Complaint save হয়েছে, কিন্তু notification email পাঠানো যায়নি।';
                            }
                            emailNotice.classList.remove('hidden');
                        }
                        closeConfirmationModal();
                        document.getElementById('complaintForm').classList.add('hidden');
                        document.getElementById('successCard').classList.remove('hidden');
                    } else {
                        alert("ত্রুটি: " + (result ? (result.message || result.error || "সফলভাবে জমা হয়নি") : "সফলভাবে জমা হয়নি"));
                    }
                })
                .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                    confirmBtn.disabled = false;
                    confirmBtn.innerText = currentLang === 'bn' ? "✓ আমি সম্মত" : "✓ I Agree";
                    alert("সার্ভার ত্রুটি: " + err.message);
                })
                .submitComplaint(record);
        }

        function resetPortal() {
            document.getElementById('complaintForm').reset();
            uploadedFiles = [];
            renderFilePreviews();
            toggleOtherInput(false);
            document.getElementById('complaintForm').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('successCard').classList.add('hidden');
            const emailNotice = document.getElementById('emailSendNotice');
            if (emailNotice) emailNotice.classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function searchComplaintStatus(e) {
            if (e) e.preventDefault();
            const query = document.getElementById('trackIdInput').value.trim();
            const resCard = document.getElementById('trackResultCard');
            const resultsList = document.getElementById('resultsList');
            
            if (!query) {
                alert("অনুগ্রহ করে একটি কমপ্লেন আইডি বা মোবাইল নম্বর লিখুন।");
                return;
            }

            const searchBtn = document.querySelector('#trackTab button');
            const originalText = searchBtn.innerText;
            searchBtn.innerText = "খোঁজা হচ্ছে...";
            searchBtn.disabled = true;

            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(function(response) {
                    searchBtn.innerText = originalText;
                    searchBtn.disabled = false;

                    if (response && response.success && response.data.length > 0) {
                        resultsList.innerHTML = '';
                        response.data.forEach(function(item) {
                            let badgeStyle = "bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-300";
                            if (item.status === "Resolved") badgeStyle = "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300";
                            if (item.status === "Rejected") badgeStyle = "bg-red-50 text-red-800 border-red-300";
                            if (item.status === "In Progress") badgeStyle = "bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 border-blue-300";

                            const cardHtml = `
                                <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm border border-slate-200/80 space-y-4">
                                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
                                        <div>
                                            <span class="text-[13px] font-black text-slate-500 block tracking-wider uppercase">COMPLAINT ID</span>
                                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                <span class="text-lg sm:text-xl font-black text-cb-black">${item.id}</span>
                                                <button onclick="copyToClipboard('${item.id}', true)" title="Copy ID" class="text-sm text-slate-400 hover:text-cb-black">
                                                    <i class="fa-regular fa-copy"></i>
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                        <span class="${badgeStyle} text-xs font-black px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full border">${item.status}</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-2xl text-sm border border-slate-100 space-y-1">
                                        <strong class="text-slate-700 block text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider">Remarks / সর্বশেষ স্ট্যাটাস নোট:</strong>
                                        <p class="text-slate-700 text-base leading-relaxed font-medium">${item.remarks || 'কোনো মন্তব্য দেওয়া হয়নি।'}</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            `;
                            resultsList.innerHTML += cardHtml;
                        });
                        resCard.classList.remove('hidden');
                    } else {
                        alert(response ? response.message : 'এই তথ্য দিয়ে কোনো কমপ্লেন পাওয়া যায়নি।');
                        resCard.classList.add('hidden');
                    }
                })
                .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                    searchBtn.innerText = originalText;
                    searchBtn.disabled = false;
                    alert('সার্ভার ত্রুটি: ' + err.message);
                })
                .trackComplaint(query);
        }

        // ============================================================
        // DASHBOARD SYSTEM
        // ============================================================

        let dashboardLoggedEmail = '';
        let dashboardUpdatingId = '';
        let dashboardUpdatingRow = 0;
        let dashboardUpdatingOriginalStatus = '';

        function showDashboardLoginError(message) {
            const box = document.getElementById('dashLoginError');
            box.innerText = message || 'লগইন করা যায়নি।';
            box.classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function setDashboardLoginLoading(isLoading) {
            const btn = document.getElementById('dashLoginBtn');
            if (!btn) return;

            btn.disabled = isLoading;
            btn.innerHTML = isLoading
                ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i><span>যাচাই করা হচ্ছে...</span>'
                : '<i class="fa-solid fa-right-to-bracket"></i><span id="dashLoginBtnSpan">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'ড্যাশবোর্ডে প্রবেশ করুন' : 'Enter Dashboard') + '</span>';
        }

        function loadDashboardData() {
            const emailInput = document.getElementById('dashEmailInput');
            const email = String(emailInput.value || '')
                .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, '')
                .replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ')
                .trim()
                .toLowerCase();

            if (!email) {
                showDashboardLoginError('অনুগ্রহ করে আপনার ইমেইল প্রদান করুন।');
                emailInput.focus();
                return;
            }

            if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
                showDashboardLoginError('সঠিক ইমেইল ঠিকানা প্রদান করুন।');
                emailInput.focus();
                return;
            }

            document.getElementById('dashLoginError').classList.add('hidden');
            setDashboardLoginLoading(true);

            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
                    setDashboardLoginLoading(false);

                    if (res && res.success) {
                        dashboardLoggedEmail = email;
                        localStorage.setItem('carrybee_dash_email', email);
                        globalDashboardPayload = res;

                        document.getElementById('dashLoginCard').classList.add('hidden');
                        document.getElementById('dashMainPanel').classList.remove('hidden');
                        document.getElementById('loggedUserEmailDisplay').innerText = email;

                        document.getElementById('topHeaderBlock').classList.add('hidden');
                        if(document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab')) document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab').classList.add('hidden');
                        if(document.getElementById('btnTrackTab')) document.getElementById('btnTrackTab').classList.add('hidden');
                        document.getElementById('tabNavContainer').classList.add('hidden');
                        switchTab('dashboardTab');

                        const roleBadge = document.getElementById('roleBadge');

                        if (res.isSuperAdmin) {
                            roleBadge.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-crown mr-1"></i> Super Admin';
                            roleBadge.className = 'bg-amber-400 text-black border-amber-500/30 text-xs font-black px-3 py-0.5 rounded-full';
                        } else {
                            const teams = Array.isArray(res.allowedTeams) ? res.allowedTeams : [];
                            roleBadge.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-users mr-1"></i> ' + escapeClientText(teams.join(', '));
                            roleBadge.className = 'bg-white/15 text-white border-white/20 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-0.5 rounded-full';
                        }

                        populateDashboardTeamFilter(res.availableTeams || res.allowedTeams || []);
                        renderTeamOverviewGrid();
                        filterDashboardTable();

                    } else {
                        document.getElementById('topHeaderBlock').classList.remove('hidden');
                        document.getElementById('tabNavContainer').classList.remove('hidden');
                        document.getElementById('tabNavContainer').classList.add('grid-cols-2');
                        if(document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab')) document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab').classList.remove('hidden');
                        if(document.getElementById('btnTrackTab')) document.getElementById('btnTrackTab').classList.remove('hidden');
                        switchTab('dashboardTab');

                        showDashboardLoginError(
                            res && res.message
                                ? res.message
                                : 'এই ইমেইলের Dashboard access পাওয়া যায়নি।'
                        );
                    }
                })
                .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                    setDashboardLoginLoading(false);
                    document.getElementById('topHeaderBlock').classList.remove('hidden');
                    document.getElementById('tabNavContainer').classList.remove('hidden');
                    document.getElementById('tabNavContainer').classList.add('grid-cols-2');
                    if(document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab')) document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab').classList.remove('hidden');
                    if(document.getElementById('btnTrackTab')) document.getElementById('btnTrackTab').classList.remove('hidden');
                    switchTab('dashboardTab');
                    showDashboardLoginError('সার্ভার ত্রুটি: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : 'Unknown error'));
                })
                .getDashboardData(email);
        }

        function refreshDashboard() {
            if (!dashboardLoggedEmail) {
                dashboardLoggedEmail = String(document.getElementById('dashEmailInput').value || '').replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
            }

            if (!dashboardLoggedEmail) return;

            const btn = document.getElementById('dashRefreshBtn');
            const oldHtml = btn.innerHTML;

            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i><span>Loading...</span>';

            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
                    btn.disabled = false;
                    btn.innerHTML = oldHtml;

                    if (res && res.success) {
                        globalDashboardPayload = res;

                        const currentSelectedTeam = document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter').value;
                        const currentSelectedStatus = document.getElementById('dashStatusFilter').value;
                        populateDashboardTeamFilter(res.availableTeams || res.allowedTeams || []);

                        if (currentSelectedTeam && Array.from(document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter').options).some(function(o) {
                            return o.value === currentSelectedTeam;
                        })) {
                            document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter').value = currentSelectedTeam;
                        }
                        if (currentSelectedStatus) {
                            document.getElementById('dashStatusFilter').value = currentSelectedStatus;
                        }

                        renderTeamOverviewGrid();
                        filterDashboardTable();
                        showDashboardToast('Dashboard refresh হয়েছে।', 'success');
                    } else {
                        showDashboardToast(res && res.message ? res.message : 'Dashboard refresh করা যায়নি।', 'error');
                    }
                })
                .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                    btn.disabled = false;
                    btn.innerHTML = oldHtml;
                    showDashboardToast('সার্ভার ত্রুটি: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : 'Unknown error'), 'error');
                })
                .getDashboardData(dashboardLoggedEmail);
        }

        function populateDashboardTeamFilter(teams) {
            const select = document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter');
            const currentValue = select.value;

            const uniqueTeams = [];
            const seen = {};

            (teams || []).forEach(function(team) {
                const clean = String(team || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
                const key = clean.toLowerCase();

                if (clean && !seen[key]) {
                    seen[key] = true;
                    uniqueTeams.push(clean);
                }
            });

            uniqueTeams.sort(function(a, b) {
                return a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
            });

            select.innerHTML = `<option value="" id="optAllTeams">${translations[currentLang].optAllTeams}</option>`;

            uniqueTeams.forEach(function(team) {
                const option = document.createElement('option');
                option.value = team;
                option.textContent = team;
                select.appendChild(option);
            });

            if (currentValue && uniqueTeams.some(function(team) { return team === currentValue; })) {
                select.value = currentValue;
            }
        }

        function renderTeamOverviewGrid() {
            const grid = document.getElementById('teamOverviewGrid');
            const countDisplay = document.getElementById('teamCardsCount');
            grid.innerHTML = '';

            if (!globalDashboardPayload || !Array.isArray(globalDashboardPayload.data)) {
                countDisplay.innerText = "0";
                return;
            }

            const rawTeams = globalDashboardPayload.availableTeams || globalDashboardPayload.allowedTeams || [];
            const activeTeamFilter = document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter').value;

            const teamPendingCounts = {};
            const teamTotalCounts = {};

            globalDashboardPayload.data.forEach(item => {
                const teamName = normalizeClientTeam(item.team);
                const status = String(item.status || '').trim().toLowerCase();

                if (!teamTotalCounts[teamName]) teamTotalCounts[teamName] = 0;
                if (!teamPendingCounts[teamName]) teamPendingCounts[teamName] = 0;

                teamTotalCounts[teamName]++;
                if (status === 'pending') {
                    teamPendingCounts[teamName]++;
                }
            });

            countDisplay.innerText = rawTeams.length;

            rawTeams.forEach(team => {
                const cleanName = String(team || '').trim();
                const normName = normalizeClientTeam(cleanName);

                const pendingCount = teamPendingCounts[normName] || 0;
                const totalCount = teamTotalCounts[normName] || 0;

                const isSelected = activeTeamFilter && normalizeClientTeam(activeTeamFilter) === normName;

                const cardHtml = `
                    <div 
                        onclick="selectTeamCard('${escapeClientAttribute(cleanName)}')" 
                        class="team-card cursor-pointer bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 border border-slate-200/80 hover:border-cb-yellow hover:shadow-lg transition-all duration-200 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-3 ${isSelected ? 'active' : ''}"
                    >
                        <div>
                            <div class="mb-2">
                                <span class="team-name-chip bg-cb-black text-white font-black tracking-wide" title="${escapeClientAttribute(cleanName)}">
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-users text-[10px]"></i><span class="team-name-text">${escapeClientText(cleanName)}</span>
                                </span>
                                ${pendingCount > 0 
                                    ? `<span class="team-pending-badge"><span class="team-pending-dot"></span><span>${currentLang === 'bn' ? `${pendingCount} টি Pending` : `${pendingCount} Pending`}</span></span>`
                                    : `<span class="team-clear-badge"><i class="fa-solid fa-check" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'Clear' : 'Clear'}</span></span>`
                                }
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs pt-2 border-t border-slate-100/80 font-bold text-slate-600">
                            <span>${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'মোট কমপ্লেন:' : 'Total:'} <strong class="text-slate-900 text-sm font-black">${totalCount}</strong></span>
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-right-long text-slate-300"></i>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
                grid.innerHTML += cardHtml;
            });
        }

        function selectTeamCard(teamName) {
            const filterSelect = document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter');
            
            if (filterSelect.value === teamName) {
                filterSelect.value = '';
            } else {
                filterSelect.value = teamName;
            }

            renderTeamOverviewGrid();
            filterDashboardTable();
        }

        function onTeamSelectChange() {
            renderTeamOverviewGrid();
            filterDashboardTable();
        }

        function onStatusFilterChange() {
            filterDashboardTable();
        }

        function updateDashboardStatsCards(filteredList) {
            let pendingCount = 0;
            let inProgressCount = 0;
            let resolvedCount = 0;
            let rejectedCount = 0;

            const total = filteredList ? filteredList.length : 0;

            if (total > 0) {
                filteredList.forEach(item => {
                    const status = String(item.status || '').trim().toLowerCase();
                    if (status === 'resolved') resolvedCount++;
                    else if (status === 'rejected') rejectedCount++;
                    else if (status === 'in progress') inProgressCount++;
                    else pendingCount++;
                });
            }

            const calcPct = (count) => total > 0 ? Math.round((count / total) * 100) : 0;

            document.getElementById('statPending').innerText = pendingCount;
            document.getElementById('pctPending').innerText = calcPct(pendingCount) + '%';

            document.getElementById('statInProgress').innerText = inProgressCount;
            document.getElementById('pctInProgress').innerText = calcPct(inProgressCount) + '%';

            document.getElementById('statResolved').innerText = resolvedCount;
            document.getElementById('pctResolved').innerText = calcPct(resolvedCount) + '%';

            document.getElementById('statRejected').innerText = rejectedCount;
            document.getElementById('pctRejected').innerText = calcPct(rejectedCount) + '%';
        }

        function filterDashboardTable() {
            if (!globalDashboardPayload || !Array.isArray(globalDashboardPayload.data)) {
                updateDashboardStatsCards([]);
                renderDashboardTable([]);
                return;
            }

            const query = (document.getElementById('dashSearchInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            const selectedTeam = document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter').value || '';
            const selectedStatus = document.getElementById('dashStatusFilter').value || '';

            const filtered = globalDashboardPayload.data.filter(function(item) {
                const id = String(item.id || '').toLowerCase();
                const name = String(item.name || '').toLowerCase();
                const mobile = String(item.mobile || '').toLowerCase();
                const problem = String(item.problem || '').toLowerCase();
                const team = String(item.team || '');

                const matchQuery =
                    !query ||
                    id.includes(query) ||
                    name.includes(query) ||
                    mobile.includes(query) ||
                    problem.includes(query);

                const matchTeam =
                    !selectedTeam ||
                    normalizeClientTeam(team) === normalizeClientTeam(selectedTeam);

                const matchStatus =
                    !selectedStatus ||
                    String(item.status || '').trim().toLowerCase() === selectedStatus.toLowerCase();

                return matchQuery && matchTeam && matchStatus;
            });

            updateDashboardStatsCards(filtered);
            renderDashboardTable(filtered);
        }

        function clearDashboardFilters() {
            document.getElementById('dashSearchInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter').value = '';
            document.getElementById('dashStatusFilter').value = '';
            renderTeamOverviewGrid();
            filterDashboardTable();
        }

        function renderDashboardTable(dataList) {
            const container = document.getElementById('dashComplaintList');
            const countEl = document.getElementById('dashResultCount');

            container.innerHTML = '';

            const total = Array.isArray(dataList) ? dataList.length : 0;
            countEl.innerText = total;

            if (!dataList || dataList.length === 0) {
                const hasFilters =
                    (document.getElementById('dashSearchInput').value || '').trim() ||
                    document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter').value ||
                    document.getElementById('dashStatusFilter').value;

                container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="bg-white rounded-3xl p-8 sm:p-12 text-center shadow-xs border border-slate-200/80">
                        <div class="w-16 h-16 mx-auto rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl mb-3">
                            <i class="fa-solid fa-${hasFilters ? 'filter-circle-xmark' : 'inbox'}"></i>
                        </div>
                        <h3 class="text-lg font-black text-slate-800">
                            ${hasFilters ? (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'এই ফিল্টারে কোনো অভিযোগ পাওয়া যায়নি' : 'No complaints found in this filter') : (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'কোনো অভিযোগ পাওয়া যায়নি' : 'No complaints found')}
                        </h3>
                        <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
                            ${hasFilters ? (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'Search, Team বা Status filter পরিবর্তন করে আবার চেষ্টা করুন।' : 'Try changing your filters.') : (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'এই access-এর অধীনে বর্তমানে কোনো অভিযোগ নেই।' : 'No complaints under this access.')}
                        </p>
                        ${hasFilters ? `
                            <button onclick="clearDashboardFilters()" class="mt-4 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-bold transition">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-rotate-left mr-1"></i> ${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'Filter পরিষ্কার করুন' : 'Clear Filter'}
                            </button>
                        ` : ''}
                    </div>
                `;
                return;
            }

            dataList.forEach(function(item) {
                const card = document.createElement('div');
                card.className = 'bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-xs border border-slate-200/80 hover:border-slate-400 hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-200 space-y-4';

                const status = String(item.status || 'Pending');
                const statusMeta = getStatusMeta(status);

                const evidenceHtml = item.evidence
                    ? `<a href="${escapeClientAttribute(item.evidence)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sky-800 hover:text-sky-950 font-extrabold text-xs bg-sky-50 hover:bg-sky-100 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-sky-200 transition">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-paperclip text-sm"></i> ${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'Evidence ফাইল দেখুন' : 'View Evidence'}
                       </a>`
                    : `<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-400 text-xs bg-slate-50 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                        <i class="fa-regular fa-file-excel"></i> ${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'Evidence নেই' : 'No Evidence'}
                       </span>`;

                const safeId = escapeClientText(item.id || '-');
                const safeDate = escapeClientText(item.date || '-');
                const safeName = escapeClientText(item.name || '-');
                const safeMobile = escapeClientText(item.mobile || '-');
                const safeTeam = escapeClientText(item.team || '-');
                const safeCategory = escapeClientText(item.category || '-');
                const safeProblem = escapeClientText(item.problem || '-');
                const safeLocation = escapeClientText(item.location || '-');
                const safeRemarks = escapeClientText(item.remarks || '');

                card.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">

                        <!-- Card Header Section -->
                        <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
                            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                                <div class="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <div class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl" title="${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'Tracking ID' : 'Tracking ID'}">
                                        <i class="fa-solid fa-hashtag text-[10px] text-slate-400"></i>
                                        <span class="text-sm font-black tracking-wider text-slate-900 break-all">${safeId}</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <button type="button" onclick="copyToClipboard('${safeId}', true)" title="${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'Tracking Number কপি করুন' : 'Copy Tracking Number'}" class="complaint-id-copy">
                                        <i class="fa-regular fa-copy"></i>
                                        <span>${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'Copy' : 'Copy'}</span>
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                                <span class="text-slate-300">•</span>
                                <span class="text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold text-slate-500"><i class="fa-regular fa-calendar-alt mr-1"></i>${safeDate}</span>
                            </div>

                            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 self-start sm:self-center ${statusMeta.badge} text-xs sm:text-sm font-black px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full border whitespace-nowrap shadow-2xs">
                                <i class="fa-solid ${statusMeta.icon}"></i>
                                ${statusMeta.label}
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        <!-- User & Meta Info -->
                        <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
                            <div>
                                <h3 class="text-lg sm:text-xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">${safeName}</h3>
                                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 mt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 font-bold">
                                    <span><i class="fa-solid fa-phone mr-1.5 text-blue-600"></i>${safeMobile}</span>
                                    <span>•</span>
                                    <span><i class="fa-solid fa-users mr-1.5 text-blue-600"></i>${safeTeam}</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- Problem & Details Box -->
                        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3.5">
                            <div class="lg:col-span-2 bg-slate-50/80 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-4">
                                <div class="text-xs font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1.5">${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'অভিযোগ / সমস্যা' : 'Complaint / Issue'}</div>
                                <div class="text-sm sm:text-base font-semibold text-slate-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line break-words">${safeProblem}</div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="bg-slate-50/80 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-4 space-y-3">
                                <div>
                                    <div class="text-xs font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">Category</div>
                                    <div class="text-sm font-black text-slate-800 break-words">${safeCategory}</div>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <div class="text-xs font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'কার বিরুদ্ধে / স্থান' : 'Against / Location'}</div>
                                    <div class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 break-words">${safeLocation}</div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="pt-1">${evidenceHtml}</div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- Remarks Display -->
                        <div class="rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/50 p-4">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black text-amber-900 mb-1">
                                <i class="fa-regular fa-comment-dots text-sm"></i> ${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'Admin Remarks / আপডেট নোট:' : 'Admin Remarks / Update Note:'}
                            </div>
                            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line break-words">
                                ${safeRemarks || `<span class="text-slate-400 italic font-normal">${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'এখনো কোনো মন্তব্য দেওয়া হয়নি' : 'No remarks given yet'}</span>`}
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- Footer Action Button -->
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-end pt-1">
                            ${status === 'Resolved' || status === 'Rejected' ? `
                                <button type="button" disabled class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-200 text-slate-500 text-xs sm:text-sm font-black cursor-not-allowed border border-slate-300">
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-lock"></i> ${status} — ${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'আর Update করা যাবে না' : 'Cannot be updated further'}
                                </button>
                            ` : `
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onclick="openStatusModal('${escapeClientAttribute(item.id || '')}', '${escapeClientAttribute(status)}', '${escapeClientAttribute(item.remarks || '')}', '${escapeClientAttribute(item.rowIdx || '')}')"
                                    class="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-cb-yellow hover:bg-cb-yellowDark active:scale-95 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-black transition shadow-sm"
                                >
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i>
                                    Update Status & Remarks
                                </button>
                            `}
                        </div>

                    </div>
                `;

                container.appendChild(card);
            });
        }

        function openStatusModal(complaintId, currentStatus, currentRemarks, rowNumber) {
            currentStatus = String(currentStatus || 'Pending').trim();

            if (currentStatus.toLowerCase() === 'resolved' || currentStatus.toLowerCase() === 'rejected') {
                const lockedStatus = currentStatus.toLowerCase() === 'rejected' ? 'Rejected' : 'Resolved';
                showDashboardToast('ഈ Complaint ইতোমধ্যে ' + lockedStatus + ' হয়েছে। এখন আর Status পরিবর্তন করা যাবে না।', 'error');
                return;
            }

            dashboardUpdatingId = String(complaintId || '').trim();
            dashboardUpdatingOriginalStatus = currentStatus;
            dashboardUpdatingRow = Number(rowNumber || 0);

            document.getElementById('statusModalComplaintId').innerText =
                complaintId ? ('Complaint ID: ' + complaintId) : 'Complaint ID: নেই — Row based update';
            document.getElementById('modalStatusSelect').value = currentStatus || 'Pending';
            document.getElementById('modalRemarkInput').value =
                currentRemarks === 'কোন মন্তব্য নেই' ? '' : (currentRemarks || '');

            const modal = document.getElementById('dashboardStatusModal');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');
            modal.classList.add('flex');

            setTimeout(function() {
                document.getElementById('modalStatusSelect').focus();
            }, 50);
        }

        function closeStatusModal() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('dashboardStatusModal');
            modal.classList.add('hidden');
            modal.classList.remove('flex');

            dashboardUpdatingId = '';
            dashboardUpdatingRow = 0;
            dashboardUpdatingOriginalStatus = '';
        }

        function confirmDashboardStatusUpdate() {
            if ((!dashboardUpdatingId && !dashboardUpdatingRow) || !dashboardLoggedEmail) return;

            const newStatus = document.getElementById('modalStatusSelect').value;
            const userNote = document.getElementById('modalRemarkInput').value.trim();

            const btn = document.getElementById('confirmDashboardUpdateBtn');

            if (!newStatus) {
                showDashboardToast('Status নির্বাচন করুন।', 'error');
                return;
            }

            const oldHtml = btn.innerHTML;
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Saving...';

            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
                    btn.disabled = false;
                    btn.innerHTML = oldHtml;

                    if (res && res.success) {
                        closeStatusModal();
                        showDashboardToast('✅ Status সফলভাবে আপডেট হয়েছে।', 'success');
                        refreshDashboard();
                    } else {
                        showDashboardToast(
                            res && res.message ? res.message : 'Status আপডেট হয়নি।',
                            'error'
                        );
                    }
                })
                .withFailureHandler(function(err) {
                    btn.disabled = false;
                    btn.innerHTML = oldHtml;
                    showDashboardToast('সার্ভার ত্রুটি: ' + (err && err.message ? err.message : 'Unknown error'), 'error');
                })
                .updateDashboardStatus(
                    dashboardLoggedEmail,
                    dashboardUpdatingId,
                    newStatus,
                    userNote,
                    dashboardUpdatingRow
                );
        }

        function logoutDashboard() {
            localStorage.removeItem('carrybee_dash_email');

            dashboardLoggedEmail = '';
            globalDashboardPayload = null;
            dashboardUpdatingId = '';
            dashboardUpdatingRow = 0;
            dashboardUpdatingOriginalStatus = '';

            document.getElementById('dashMainPanel').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('dashLoginCard').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('dashEmailInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('dashSearchInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('dashTeamFilter').innerHTML = `<option value="" id="optAllTeams">${translations[currentLang].optAllTeams}</option>`;
            document.getElementById('dashStatusFilter').value = '';
            document.getElementById('dashLoginError').classList.add('hidden');

            document.getElementById('topHeaderBlock').classList.remove('hidden');
            if(document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab')) document.getElementById('btnSubmitTab').classList.remove('hidden');
            if(document.getElementById('btnTrackTab')) document.getElementById('btnTrackTab').classList.remove('hidden');
            
            document.getElementById('tabNavContainer').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('tabNavContainer').classList.add('grid-cols-2');

            closeStatusModal();
            switchTab('submitTab');
        }

        function getStatusMeta(status) {
            if (status === 'Resolved') {
                return {
                    label: 'Resolved',
                    icon: 'fa-circle-check',
                    badge: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300'
                };
            }

            if (status === 'Rejected') {
                return {
                    label: 'Rejected',
                    icon: 'fa-circle-xmark',
                    badge: 'bg-red-50 text-red-800 border-red-300'
                };
            }

            if (status === 'In Progress') {
                return {
                    label: 'In Progress',
                    icon: 'fa-spinner',
                    badge: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 border-blue-300'
                };
            }

            return {
                label: 'Pending',
                icon: 'fa-clock',
                badge: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-300'
            };
        }

        function normalizeClientTeam(value) {
            return String(value || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').toLowerCase();
        }

        function escapeClientText(value) {
            return String(value == null ? '' : value)
                .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
                .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
                .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
                .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
                .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
        }

        function escapeClientAttribute(value) {
            return escapeClientText(value)
                .replace(/`/g, '&#096;');
        }

        function showDashboardToast(message, type) {
            let toast = document.getElementById('dashboardToast');

            if (!toast) {
                toast = document.createElement('div');
                toast.id = 'dashboardToast';
                toast.className = 'fixed left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bottom-5 z-[80] max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] px-6 py-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl text-sm font-black transition-all duration-300 opacity-0 translate-y-2';
                document.body.appendChild(toast);
            }

            toast.className = 'fixed left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bottom-5 z-[80] max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] px-6 py-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl text-sm font-black transition-all duration-300 opacity-100 translate-y-0 ' +
                (type === 'error'
                    ? 'bg-red-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-cb-black text-white border border-cb-yellow/30');

            toast.innerText = message;

            setTimeout(function() {
                toast.className = toast.className.replace('opacity-100 translate-y-0', 'opacity-0 translate-y-2');
            }, 3000);
        }
    </script>

    <!-- JOB OPPORTUNITY SCRIPT (UPDATED LOGIC FOR DONE BUTTON AND VIEW DETAILS TOGGLE) -->
    <script id="job-opportunity-add-on-js">
    (function(){
      "use strict";
      let JOB_jobs = [];
      let JOB_current = null;

      document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(){
        JOB_loadJobs();
        const f = document.getElementById("jobCvFile");
        if(f) f.addEventListener("change", function(){
          const file = f.files && f.files[0];
          document.getElementById("jobFileName").textContent = file ? file.name : (currentLang === 'bn' ? "সিভি বা ফাইল নির্বাচন করুন" : "Select CV or File");
        });
      });

      window.JOB_loadJobs = function(){
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
            if(!res || !res.success){
              JOB_renderError(res && res.message ? res.message : "Job data load করতে সমস্যা হয়েছে।");
              return;
            }
            JOB_jobs = res.jobs || [];
            document.getElementById("jobActiveCount").textContent = String(res.activeCount || 0);
            document.getElementById("jobFullCount").textContent = String(res.activeCount || 0);
            JOB_renderFullList();
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(){
            JOB_renderError("Job data load করতে সমস্যা হয়েছে।");
          })
          .getJobCirculars();
      };

      // Open the existing Job Circular page directly from Notice Board.
      window.JOB_enterFullViewFromNotice = function(){
        window._JOB_returnToNoticeBoard = true;
        if (window.NBOARD_exitFullView) {
          const noticeView = document.getElementById("noticeBoardFullView");
          if (noticeView) noticeView.style.display = "none";
          document.body.classList.remove("notice-board-mode");
        }
        JOB_enterFullView();
      };

      window.JOB_enterFullView = function(){
        if (typeof window._JOB_returnToNoticeBoard === "undefined") window._JOB_returnToNoticeBoard = false;
        document.body.classList.add("job-opportunity-mode");
        document.getElementById("jobOpportunityCard").style.display = "none";
        document.querySelector("main").style.display = "none";
        document.getElementById("jobOpportunityFullView").style.display = "block";
        document.getElementById("jobOpportunityFullView").scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth", block:"start"});
        JOB_renderFullList();
      };

      window.JOB_exitFullView = function(){
        JOB_closeApplication();
        document.body.classList.remove("job-opportunity-mode");
        const jobView = document.getElementById("jobOpportunityFullView");
        const jobCard = document.getElementById("jobOpportunityCard");
        const main = document.querySelector("main");
        if (jobView) jobView.style.display = "none";

        if (window._JOB_returnToNoticeBoard) {
          window._JOB_returnToNoticeBoard = false;
          if (jobCard) jobCard.style.display = "none";
          if (main) main.style.display = "none";
          document.body.classList.add("notice-board-mode");
          const noticeView = document.getElementById("noticeBoardFullView");
          if (noticeView) {
            noticeView.style.display = "block";
            noticeView.scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth", block:"start"});
          }
          if (window.NBOARD_renderFullList) window.NBOARD_renderFullList();
          return;
        }

        if (jobCard) jobCard.style.display = "";
        if (main) main.style.display = "";
        window.scrollTo({top:0, behavior:"smooth"});
      };

      function JOB_renderFullList(){
        const box = document.getElementById("jobFullList");
        if(!box) return;

        if(!JOB_jobs.length){
          box.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-12"><div class="text-4xl mb-3">💼</div><div class="font-black text-slate-800">${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'বর্তমানে কোনো পদ খালি নেই' : 'No open positions right now'}</div><div class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">${currentLang === 'bn' ? 'পরবর্তীতে আবার দেখুন।' : 'Please check back later.'}</div></div>`;
          return;
        }

        box.innerHTML = JOB_jobs.map(function(job,index){
          const preview = String(job.description || "").slice(0,190);
          const viewDetailsText = currentLang === 'bn' ? 'বিস্তারিত দেখুন' : 'View Details';
          return '<div class="job-list-item">' +
            '<div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">' +
              '<div class="min-w-0"><div class="text-lg font-black text-slate-900">' + JOB_escape(job.title) + '</div>' +
              '<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-2">' +
                (job.department ? '<span class="job-pill"><i class="fa-solid fa-building"></i>'+JOB_escape(job.department)+'</span>' : '') +
                (job.salary ? '<span class="job-pill"><i class="fa-solid fa-money-bill-wave"></i>'+JOB_escape(job.salary)+'</span>' : '') +
              '</div></div>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-6 mt-3">' + JOB_escape(preview) + (String(job.description||"").length>190?"…":"") + '</div>' +
            '<button type="button" id="jobToggleBtn_'+index+'" class="job-action w-full mt-4" onclick="JOB_toggleDetails('+index+')"><i class="fa-solid fa-eye"></i> <span>'+viewDetailsText+'</span></button>' +
            '<div id="jobDetails_'+index+'" class="job-details">' +
              '<div class="job-detail-top">' +
                '<div class="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider opacity-75 font-bold">'+(currentLang === 'bn' ? 'ক্যারিয়ার সুযোগ' : 'Career Opportunity')+'</div>' +
                '<div class="text-2xl font-black mt-1 break-words">' + JOB_escape(job.title) + '</div>' +
              '</div>' +
              '<div class="job-info-grid">' +
                '<div class="job-info-box"><div class="job-info-label">'+(currentLang === 'bn' ? 'ডিপার্টমেন্ট' : 'Department')+'</div><div class="job-info-value">'+JOB_escape(job.department||"—")+'</div></div>' +
                '<div class="job-info-box"><div class="job-info-label">'+(currentLang === 'bn' ? 'বেতন/স্যালারি' : 'Salary')+'</div><div class="job-info-value">'+JOB_escape(job.salary||"—")+'</div></div>' +
              '</div>' +
              '<div class="font-black text-slate-900 mb-2">'+(currentLang === 'bn' ? 'কাজের বিবরণ' : 'Job Description')+'</div>' +
              '<div class="job-description">'+JOB_escape(job.description||(currentLang === 'bn' ? "কোনো বিবরণ পাওয়া যায়নি。" : "No description available."))+'</div>' +
              (job.youtube ? '<div class="job-video"><div class="font-black text-slate-900 mb-3"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-play text-amber-700 mr-1"></i> '+(currentLang === 'bn' ? 'বিবরণ ভিডিও' : 'Video Overview')+'</div><div class="job-video-frame"><iframe src="'+JOB_escapeAttr(job.youtube)+'?rel=0&modestbranding=1" title="Job Video" allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share" allowfullscreen></iframe></div></div>' : '') +
              '<div class="job-apply"><div class="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center mb-3">'+(currentLang === 'bn' ? 'আবেদন করার আগে পুরো বিবরণটি মনোযোগ দিয়ে পড়ুন।' : 'Please read the details carefully before applying.')+'</div><button type="button" class="job-apply-btn" onclick="JOB_openApplication('+index+')"><i class="fa-solid fa-paper-plane mr-2"></i>'+(currentLang === 'bn' ? 'আবেদন করুন' : 'Apply Now')+'</button></div>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</div>';
        }).join("");
      }

      // TOGGLE VIEW DETAILS & HIDE DETAILS FIX
      window.JOB_toggleDetails = function(index){
        const el = document.getElementById("jobDetails_"+index);
        const btn = document.getElementById("jobToggleBtn_"+index);
        if(!el || !btn) return;
        
        const isOpen = el.classList.contains("open");
        const viewText = currentLang === 'bn' ? 'বিস্তারিত দেখুন' : 'View Details';
        const hideText = currentLang === 'bn' ? 'সংক্ষিপ্ত করুন' : 'Hide Details';
        
        if(isOpen) {
          el.classList.remove("open");
          btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-eye"></i> <span>'+viewText+'</span>';
        } else {
          document.querySelectorAll(".job-details.open").forEach(function(x){
            x.classList.remove("open");
          });
          document.querySelectorAll("[id^='jobToggleBtn_']").forEach(function(b){
            b.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-eye"></i> <span>'+viewText+'</span>';
          });
          
          el.classList.add("open");
          btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-eye-slash"></i> <span>'+hideText+'</span>';
          setTimeout(function(){ el.scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth", block:"nearest"}); }, 50);
        }
      };

      window.JOB_openApplication = function(index){
        JOB_current = JOB_jobs[index];
        if(!JOB_current) return;
        document.getElementById("jobApplyTitle").textContent = JOB_current.title + (JOB_current.department ? " • " + JOB_current.department : "");
        document.getElementById("jobApplyPosition").value = JOB_current.title;
        document.getElementById("jobApplyDepartment").value = JOB_current.department || "";
        document.getElementById("jobApplicationError").classList.add("hidden");
        document.getElementById("jobApplicationOverlay").style.display = "flex";
        document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
      };

      window.JOB_closeApplication = function(){
        const overlay = document.getElementById("jobApplicationOverlay");
        if(overlay) overlay.style.display = "none";
        document.body.style.overflow = "";
      };

      window.JOB_closeApplicationBackdrop = function(e){
        if(e.target && e.target.id === "jobApplicationOverlay") JOB_closeApplication();
      };

      window.JOB_submitApplication = function(e){
        e.preventDefault();
        const name = document.getElementById("jobApplicantName").value.trim();
        const mobile = document.getElementById("jobApplicantMobile").value.trim();
        const email = document.getElementById("jobApplicantEmail").value.trim();
        const fileInput = document.getElementById("jobCvFile");
        const file = fileInput && fileInput.files ? fileInput.files[0] : null;
        const error = document.getElementById("jobApplicationError");
        const btn = document.getElementById("jobSubmitBtn");

        error.classList.add("hidden");
        if(!name || !mobile || !email || !file){
          error.textContent = currentLang === 'bn' ? "সব প্রয়োজনীয় তথ্য পূরণ করুন এবং CV সংযুক্ত করুন।" : "Please fill all required info and attach CV.";
          error.classList.remove("hidden"); return;
        }
        const ext = (file.name.split(".").pop() || "").toLowerCase();
        if(["pdf","doc","docx"].indexOf(ext) === -1){
          error.textContent = currentLang === 'bn' ? "CV শুধু PDF, DOC অথবা DOCX format-এ দেওয়া যাবে।" : "CV must be in PDF, DOC or DOCX format.";
          error.classList.remove("hidden"); return;
        }
        if(file.size > 10*1024*1024){
          error.textContent = currentLang === 'bn' ? "CV file size সর্বোচ্চ 10 MB হতে পারবে।" : "CV file size cannot exceed 10 MB.";
          error.classList.remove("hidden"); return;
        }

        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin mr-2"></i>' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? "জমাদান হচ্ছে..." : "Submitting...");

        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = function(ev){
          const dataUrl = String(ev.target.result || "");
          const base64 = dataUrl.indexOf(",") >= 0 ? dataUrl.split(",")[1] : dataUrl;

          google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
              btn.disabled = false;
              btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-paper-plane mr-2"></i>' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? "আবেদন জমা দিন" : "Submit Application");

              if(!res || !res.success){
                error.textContent = res && res.message ? res.message : "Application submit করা যায়নি।";
                error.classList.remove("hidden"); return;
              }

              document.getElementById("jobApplicationBody").innerHTML =
                '<div class="text-center py-4">' +
                  '<div class="text-5xl text-emerald-600 mb-4"><i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i></div>' +
                  '<div class="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'আবেদন সফলভাবে জমা হয়েছে' : 'Application Submitted Successfully') + '</div>' +
                  '<div class="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-2">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'আপনার সিভি সফলভাবে পাওয়া গেছে।' : 'Your CV has been successfully received.') + '</div>' +
                  '<div class="mt-5 p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200"><div class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'অ্যাপ্লিকেশন আইডি' : 'Application ID') + '</div><div class="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1">'+JOB_escape(res.applicationId)+'</div></div>' +
                  '<button type="button" class="job-back w-full mt-5 bg-emerald-600 text-white border-0 py-3" onclick="JOB_resetApplication()">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'সম্পন্ন হয়েছে (Done)' : 'Done') + '</button>' +
                '</div>';
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err){
              btn.disabled = false;
              btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-paper-plane mr-2"></i>' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? "আবেদন জমা দিন" : "Submit Application");
              error.textContent = "Application submit করতে সমস্যা হয়েছে। আবার চেষ্টা করুন।";
              error.classList.remove("hidden");
              console.error(err);
            })
            .submitJobApplication({
              name:name, mobile:mobile, email:email,
              jobTitle:document.getElementById("jobApplyPosition").value,
              department:document.getElementById("jobApplyDepartment").value,
              file:{name:file.name,type:file.type,data:base64}
            });
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      };

      // DONE BUTTON FIX: Properly closes application dialog and resets form
      window.JOB_resetApplication = function(){
        JOB_closeApplication();
        setTimeout(function(){
            document.getElementById("jobApplicationBody").innerHTML =
              '<form id="jobApplicationForm" onsubmit="JOB_submitApplication(event)">' +
              '<input type="hidden" id="jobApplyPosition"><input type="hidden" id="jobApplyDepartment">' +
              '<div class="mb-4"><label class="block font-black text-sm text-slate-700 mb-2">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'আপনার পূর্ণ নাম *' : 'Your Full Name *') + '</label><input id="jobApplicantName" class="job-input" type="text" required placeholder="উদাহরণ: মোঃ আরিয়ান"></div>' +
              '<div class="mb-4"><label class="block font-black text-sm text-slate-700 mb-2">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'মোবাইল নম্বর *' : 'Mobile Number *') + '</label><input id="jobApplicantMobile" class="job-input" type="tel" required placeholder="01XXXXXXXXX"></div>' +
              '<div class="mb-4"><label class="block font-black text-sm text-slate-700 mb-2">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'ইমেইল ঠিকানা *' : 'Email Address *') + '</label><input id="jobApplicantEmail" class="job-input" type="email" required placeholder="example@domain.com"></div>' +
              '<div class="mb-4"><label class="block font-black text-sm text-slate-700 mb-2">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'সিভি / জীবনবৃত্তান্ত *' : 'CV / Resume *') + '</label><label for="jobCvFile" class="job-file block"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-arrow-up text-3xl text-amber-700"></i><div id="jobFileName" class="font-bold text-slate-700 mt-2">' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'সিভি বা ফাইল নির্বাচন করুন' : 'Select CV or File') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">PDF, DOC অথবা DOCX (সর্বোচ্চ ১০ এমবি)</div></label><input id="jobCvFile" type="file" accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx" class="hidden"></div>' +
              '<div id="jobApplicationError" class="job-error hidden mb-4"></div><button id="jobSubmitBtn" type="submit" class="job-submit"><i class="fa-solid fa-paper-plane mr-2"></i>' + (currentLang === 'bn' ? 'আবেদন জমা দিন' : 'Submit Application') + '</button></form>';
            
            const f = document.getElementById("jobCvFile");
            if(f) f.addEventListener("change",function(){
                const file=f.files&&f.files[0];
                document.getElementById("jobFileName").textContent=file?file.name:(currentLang === 'bn' ? "সিভি বা ফাইল নির্বাচন করুন" : "Select CV or File");
            });
        }, 300);
      };

      function JOB_renderError(msg){
        document.getElementById("jobFullList").innerHTML='<div class="text-center py-10 text-red-700 font-bold">'+JOB_escape(msg)+'</div>';
      }
      function JOB_escape(v){return String(v==null?"":v).replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;").replace(/>/g,"&gt;").replace(/"/g,"&quot;").replace(/'/g,"&#039;");}
      function JOB_escapeAttr(v){return JOB_escape(v).replace(/`/g,"&#096;");}
    })();
    </script>


    <!-- NOTICE BOARD ADD-ON: SCRIPT -->
    <script id="notice-board-add-on-js">
    (function(){
        "use strict";

        let NBOARD_items = [];
        let NBOARD_activeDetailIndex = -1;

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(){
            NBOARD_load();
        });

        window.NBOARD_load = function(){
            google.script.run
                .withSuccessHandler(function(res){
                    if(!res || !res.success){
                        NBOARD_renderError(res && res.message ? res.message : "Notice Board data load করতে সমস্যা হয়েছে।");
                        return;
                    }
                    NBOARD_items = Array.isArray(res.notices) ? res.notices : [];
                    const count = document.getElementById("noticeBoardCount");
                    if(count) count.textContent = String(NBOARD_items.length);
                    NBOARD_renderFullList();
                })
                .withFailureHandler(function(err){
                    NBOARD_renderError("Notice Board data load করতে সমস্যা হয়েছে।");
                })
                .getNoticeBoardData();
        };

        window.NBOARD_enterFullView = function(){
            document.body.classList.remove("dashboard-mode");
            const noticeView = document.getElementById("noticeBoardFullView");
            const jobCard = document.getElementById("jobOpportunityCard");
            const jobView = document.getElementById("jobOpportunityFullView");
            const main = document.querySelector("main");
            if(!noticeView) return;

            if(jobCard) jobCard.style.display = "none";
            if(jobView) jobView.style.display = "none";
            if(main) main.style.display = "none";

            document.body.classList.add("notice-board-mode");
            noticeView.style.display = "block";
            NBOARD_renderFullList();
            noticeView.scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth", block:"start"});
        };

        window.NBOARD_exitFullView = function(){
            const noticeView = document.getElementById("noticeBoardFullView");
            const jobCard = document.getElementById("jobOpportunityCard");
            const main = document.querySelector("main");

            document.body.classList.remove("notice-board-mode");
            if(noticeView) noticeView.style.display = "none";
            if(jobCard) jobCard.style.display = "";
            if(main) main.style.display = "";
            window.scrollTo({top:0, behavior:"smooth"});
        };

        window.NBOARD_renderFullList = function(){
            const box = document.getElementById("noticeBoardList");
            if(!box) return;

            const bn = typeof currentLang !== "undefined" ? currentLang === "bn" : true;
            const count = document.getElementById("noticeBoardCount");
            if(count) count.textContent = String(NBOARD_items.length);

            if(!NBOARD_items.length){
                box.innerHTML =
                    '<div class="notice-empty">' +
                        '<div class="notice-empty-icon"><i class="fa-regular fa-bell-slash"></i></div>' +
                        '<div class="font-black text-slate-800">' +
                            (bn ? 'এই মুহূর্তে কোনো সক্রিয় নোটিশ নেই' : 'No active notices right now') +
                        '</div>' +
                        '<div class="text-sm mt-1">' +
                            (bn ? 'নতুন নোটিশ প্রকাশ হলে এখানে দেখা যাবে।' : 'New notices will appear here when published.') +
                        '</div>' +
                    '</div>';
                return;
            }

            box.innerHTML = NBOARD_items.map(function(item){
                const safeTitle = NBOARD_escape(item.title || (bn ? "শিরোনাম নেই" : "Untitled Notice"));
                const fullDescription = String(item.description || (bn ? "কোনো বিবরণ নেই।" : "No description available."));
                const safeDate = NBOARD_escape(item.date || "—");
                const preview = NBOARD_escape(NBOARD_makePreview(fullDescription, 145));
                const link = String(item.downloadLink || "").trim();
                const idx = NBOARD_items.indexOf(item);

                // View always opens our own detail modal. It never opens the Drive link.
                // Download button is rendered ONLY when a link exists.
                const downloadButton = link
                    ? '<a class="notice-btn notice-btn-download" href="' + NBOARD_escapeAttr(link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">' +
                        '<i class="fa-solid fa-download"></i>' + (bn ? ' ডাউনলোড' : ' Download') +
                      '</a>'
                    : '';

                const actions = '<div class="notice-actions">' +
                    '<button type="button" class="notice-btn notice-btn-view" onclick="NBOARD_openDetail(' + idx + ')">' +
                        '<i class="fa-regular fa-eye"></i>' + (bn ? ' বিস্তারিত দেখুন' : ' View Details') +
                    '</button>' +
                    (downloadButton ? downloadButton : '') +
                    '</div>';

                return '<article class="notice-item">' +
                    '<div class="notice-date"><i class="fa-regular fa-calendar"></i>' + safeDate + '</div>' +
                    '<div class="notice-title">' + safeTitle + '</div>' +
                    '<div class="notice-description">' + preview + '</div>' +
                    actions +
                '</article>';
            }).join("");
        };

        function NBOARD_makePreview(text, maxLength){
            const clean = String(text || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
            if(clean.length <= maxLength) return clean;
            return clean.slice(0, maxLength).replace(/\s+$/, "") + "…";
        }

        window.NBOARD_refreshDetailLanguage = function(){
            if (NBOARD_activeDetailIndex < 0) return;
            const item = NBOARD_items[NBOARD_activeDetailIndex];
            const actions = document.getElementById('noticeModalActions');
            if (!item || !actions) return;
            const bn = typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' ? currentLang === 'bn' : true;
            const link = String(item.downloadLink || '').trim();
            actions.innerHTML = link
                ? '<a class="notice-modal-download" href="' + NBOARD_escapeAttr(link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><i class="fa-solid fa-download"></i>' + (bn ? ' ডাউনলোড' : ' Download') + '</a>'
                : '';
        };

        window.NBOARD_openDetail = function(index){
            const item = NBOARD_items[index];
            const modal = document.getElementById("noticeDetailModal");
            if(!item || !modal) return;

            NBOARD_activeDetailIndex = index;
            const bn = typeof currentLang !== "undefined" ? currentLang === "bn" : true;
            document.getElementById("noticeModalDate").innerHTML = '<i class="fa-regular fa-calendar mr-1"></i>' + NBOARD_escape(item.date || "—");
