            document.getElementById("noticeModalTitle").textContent = item.title || (bn ? "শিরোনাম নেই" : "Untitled Notice");
            document.getElementById("noticeModalDescription").textContent = item.description || (bn ? "কোনো বিবরণ নেই।" : "No description available.");

            const actions = document.getElementById("noticeModalActions");
            const link = String(item.downloadLink || "").trim();
            actions.innerHTML = link
                ? '<a class="notice-modal-download" href="' + NBOARD_escapeAttr(link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><i class="fa-solid fa-download"></i>' + (bn ? ' ডাউনলোড' : ' Download') + '</a>'
                : '';

            modal.classList.add("is-open");
            modal.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "false");
            document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
        };

        window.NBOARD_closeDetail = function(){
            const modal = document.getElementById("noticeDetailModal");
            if(!modal) return;
            modal.classList.remove("is-open");
            modal.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
            NBOARD_activeDetailIndex = -1;
            document.body.style.overflow = "";
        };

        document.addEventListener("keydown", function(e){
            if(e.key === "Escape") NBOARD_closeDetail();
        });

        function NBOARD_renderError(message){
            const box = document.getElementById("noticeBoardList");
            if(!box) return;
            box.innerHTML =
                '<div class="notice-empty">' +
                    '<div class="notice-empty-icon"><i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation"></i></div>' +
                    '<div class="font-black text-red-700">' + NBOARD_escape(message) + '</div>' +
                '</div>';
        }

        function NBOARD_escape(value){
            return String(value == null ? "" : value)
                .replace(/&/g,"&amp;")
                .replace(/</g,"&lt;")
                .replace(/>/g,"&gt;")
                .replace(/"/g,"&quot;")
                .replace(/'/g,"&#039;");
        }

        function NBOARD_escapeAttr(value){
            return NBOARD_escape(value).replace(/`/g,"&#096;");
        }
    })();
    </script>

<div id="contactUsOverlay" aria-hidden="true" onclick="CONTACT_backdrop(event)">
  <div class="contact-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="contactModalTitle">
    <div class="contact-modal-head">
      <div>
        <div id="contactModalTitle" class="contact-modal-title"><i class="fa-solid fa-address-book mr-2"></i>Contact Us</div>
        <div class="contact-modal-sub">Department নির্বাচন করে সংশ্লিষ্ট Active person-এর contact information দেখুন</div>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="contact-close" onclick="CONTACT_close()" aria-label="Close"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i></button>
    </div>
    <div class="contact-modal-body">
      <div class="contact-dept-wrap">
        <label for="contactDepartmentSelect" class="contact-dept-label">Select Department</label>
        <select id="contactDepartmentSelect" onchange="CONTACT_filter()"><option value="">-- Select Department --</option></select>
      </div>
      <div id="contactPersonGrid"><div class="contact-loading"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-xl mb-2"></i><div>Contact information loading...</div></div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script id="contact-us-add-on-js">
(function(){
"use strict";
let CONTACT_items=[],CONTACT_loaded=false;
window.CONTACT_open=function(){const o=document.getElementById("contactUsOverlay");if(!o)return;o.classList.add("show");o.setAttribute("aria-hidden","false");document.body.style.overflow="hidden";if(!CONTACT_loaded)CONTACT_load()};
window.CONTACT_close=function(){const o=document.getElementById("contactUsOverlay");if(!o)return;o.classList.remove("show");o.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");document.body.style.overflow=""};
window.CONTACT_backdrop=function(e){if(e&&e.target&&e.target.id==="contactUsOverlay")CONTACT_close()};
window.CONTACT_load=function(){const g=document.getElementById("contactPersonGrid");if(g)g.innerHTML='<div class="contact-loading"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin text-xl mb-2"></i><div>Contact information loading...</div></div>';google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(!res||!res.success){CONTACT_empty(res&&res.message?res.message:"Contact information load করা যায়নি।");return}CONTACT_items=Array.isArray(res.contacts)?res.contacts:[];CONTACT_loaded=true;CONTACT_departments(res.departments||[]);CONTACT_filter()}).withFailureHandler(function(err){CONTACT_empty("Contact information load করতে সমস্যা হয়েছে।");console.error(err)}).getContactDirectory()};
function CONTACT_departments(ds){const s=document.getElementById("contactDepartmentSelect");if(!s)return;s.innerHTML='<option value="">-- Select Department --</option>';ds.forEach(function(d){const o=document.createElement("option");o.value=d;o.textContent=d;s.appendChild(o)})}
window.CONTACT_filter=function(){const s=document.getElementById("contactDepartmentSelect"),g=document.getElementById("contactPersonGrid");if(!g)return;const dep=s?String(s.value||"").trim():"";if(!dep){CONTACT_empty("Department নির্বাচন করুন, তারপর সংশ্লিষ্ট contact person দেখা যাবে।");return}const list=CONTACT_items.filter(function(x){return CONTACT_key(x.department)===CONTACT_key(dep)});if(!list.length){CONTACT_empty("এই Department-এ কোনো Active contact পাওয়া যায়নি।");return}g.innerHTML=list.map(CONTACT_card).join("")};
function CONTACT_card(x){const photo=CONTACT_attr(x.photo||""),name=CONTACT_escape(x.name),des=CONTACT_escape(x.designation||""),mobile=CONTACT_escape(x.mobile||""),wn=CONTACT_wa(x.whatsapp||"") ,call=x.mobile?"tel:"+encodeURIComponent(x.mobile):"#",wh=wn||"#";const ph=photo?'<img class="contact-photo" src="'+photo+'" alt="'+name+'" loading="lazy" onerror="this.style.display=\'none\';this.nextElementSibling.style.display=\'flex\';"><div class="contact-photo contact-photo-fallback" style="display:none;">'+CONTACT_initial(x.name)+'</div>':'<div class="contact-photo contact-photo-fallback">'+CONTACT_initial(x.name)+'</div>';return '<article class="contact-person-card">'+ph+'<div class="contact-name">'+name+'</div><div class="contact-designation">'+des+'</div><div class="contact-actions">'+(mobile?'<a class="contact-action-btn contact-call" href="'+call+'"><i class="fa-solid fa-phone"></i> Call</a>':'<span class="contact-action-btn opacity-50">Call unavailable</span>')+(wn?'<a class="contact-action-btn contact-whatsapp" href="'+wh+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fa-brands fa-whatsapp"></i> WhatsApp</a>':'<span class="contact-action-btn opacity-50">WhatsApp unavailable</span>')+'</div></article>'}
function CONTACT_empty(m){const g=document.getElementById("contactPersonGrid");if(g)g.innerHTML='<div class="contact-empty"><i class="fa-solid fa-users text-3xl text-slate-300 mb-3"></i><div class="font-black">'+CONTACT_escape(m)+'</div></div>'}
function CONTACT_key(v){return String(v||"").trim().replace(/\s+/g," ").toLowerCase()}
function CONTACT_initial(v){const t=String(v||"?").trim();return CONTACT_escape(t?t.charAt(0).toUpperCase():"?")}
function CONTACT_wa(v){
  const raw=String(v||"").trim();
  if(!raw)return"";
  if(/^https?:\/\/(?:wa\.me|www\.wa\.me|api\.whatsapp\.com|chat\.whatsapp\.com)\//i.test(raw))return raw;
  let n=raw.replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
  if(n.length>=10){
    if(n.indexOf("880")===0)return"https://wa.me/"+n;
    if(n.indexOf("0")===0&&n.length===11)return"https://wa.me/880"+n.substring(1);
    return"https://wa.me/"+n;
  }
  let username=raw.replace(/^@+/,"").trim();
  username=username.replace(/\s+/g,"");
  if(username)return"https://wa.me/"+encodeURIComponent(username);
  return"";
}
function CONTACT_escape(v){return String(v==null?"":v).replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;").replace(/>/g,"&gt;").replace(/"/g,"&quot;").replace(/'/g,"&#039;")}
function CONTACT_attr(v){return CONTACT_escape(v).replace(/`/g,"&#096;")}

window.ANON_open=function(){
    const o=document.getElementById("anonymousComplaintOverlay");
    if(!o)return;
    o.classList.remove("hidden");
    document.body.style.overflow="hidden";
};

window.ANON_close=function(){
    const o=document.getElementById("anonymousComplaintOverlay");
    if(!o)return;
    o.classList.add("hidden");
    document.body.style.overflow="";
    ANON_closeConfirmation();
};

window.ANON_openConfirmation=function(e){
    if(e)e.preventDefault();

    const categoryEl=document.querySelector('input[name="anonymousCategory"]:checked');
    let category=categoryEl?String(categoryEl.value||"").trim():"";
    const details=String(document.getElementById("anonymousComplaintDetails").value||"").trim();
    const person=String(document.getElementById("anonymousPersonInvolved").value||"").trim();

    if(!category){
        alert("অনুগ্রহ করে ক্যাটাগরি নির্বাচন করুন।");
        return;
    }

    if(category==="অন্যান্য (Other)"){
        const other=String(document.getElementById("anonOtherCategoryText").value||"").trim();
        if(!other){
            alert("অনুগ্রহ করে আপনার ক্যাটাগরি লিখুন।");
            document.getElementById("anonOtherCategoryText").focus();
            return;
        }
        category="অন্যান্য: "+other;
    }

    if(!details){
        alert("অনুগ্রহ করে বিস্তারিত বিবরণ লিখুন।");
        document.getElementById("anonymousComplaintDetails").focus();
        return;
    }

    document.getElementById("anonConfirmCategory").innerText=category;
    document.getElementById("anonConfirmPerson").innerText=person||"উল্লেখ করা হয়নি";
    document.getElementById("anonConfirmDetails").innerText=details;

    const evidenceText=anonymousUploadedFile
        ? anonymousUploadedFile.name+" ("+(anonymousUploadedFile.size/(1024*1024)).toFixed(2)+" MB)"
        : "কোনো প্রমাণ সংযুক্ত করা হয়নি";
    document.getElementById("anonConfirmEvidence").innerText=evidenceText;

    document.getElementById("anonymousConfirmationModal").classList.remove("hidden");
};

window.ANON_closeConfirmation=function(){
    const o=document.getElementById("anonymousConfirmationModal");
    if(o)o.classList.add("hidden");
};

window.ANON_cancelFromConfirmation=function(){
    ANON_closeConfirmation();
    ANON_close();
    const form=document.getElementById("anonymousComplaintForm");
    if(form)form.reset();
    ANON_removeFile();
    ANON_toggleOther(false);
};

window.ANON_toggleOther=function(show){
    const box=document.getElementById("anonOtherCategoryBox");
    if(box)box.classList.toggle("hidden",!show);
    if(!show){
        const input=document.getElementById("anonOtherCategoryText");
        if(input)input.value="";
    }
};

let anonymousUploadedFile=null;

window.ANON_handleFile=function(event){
    const input=event&&event.target;
    const file=input&&input.files&&input.files[0];
    if(!file)return;

    const maxMB=10;
    if(file.size>maxMB*1024*1024){
        alert("File size সর্বোচ্চ 10 MB হতে পারবে।");
        input.value="";
        return;
    }

    anonymousUploadedFile=file;
    document.getElementById("anonFileName").innerText=file.name;
    document.getElementById("anonFileSize").innerText="("+(file.size/(1024*1024)).toFixed(2)+" MB)";
    document.getElementById("anonFilePreview").classList.remove("hidden");
};

window.ANON_removeFile=function(){
    anonymousUploadedFile=null;
    const input=document.getElementById("anonFileUpload");
    if(input)input.value="";
    const preview=document.getElementById("anonFilePreview");
    if(preview)preview.classList.add("hidden");
    const name=document.getElementById("anonFileName");
    const size=document.getElementById("anonFileSize");
    if(name)name.innerText="";
    if(size)size.innerText="";
};

window.ANON_confirmAndSubmit=async function(){
    const btn=document.getElementById("anonConfirmSubmitBtn");
    if(!btn)return;

    btn.disabled=true;
    btn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i><span>জমা হচ্ছে...</span>';

    try{
        const categoryEl=document.querySelector('input[name="anonymousCategory"]:checked');
        let category=categoryEl?String(categoryEl.value||"").trim():"";
        if(category==="অন্যান্য (Other)"){
            category="অন্যান্য: "+String(document.getElementById("anonOtherCategoryText").value||"").trim();
        }

        const details=String(document.getElementById("anonymousComplaintDetails").value||"").trim();
        const person=String(document.getElementById("anonymousPersonInvolved").value||"").trim();

        let fileData=null;
        if(anonymousUploadedFile){
            const base64=await convertBase64(anonymousUploadedFile);
            fileData={
                name:anonymousUploadedFile.name,
                type:anonymousUploadedFile.type,
                data:base64.split(",")[1]
            };
        }

        const record={
            category:category,
            problem:details,
            location:person||"N/A",
            confirmed:true,
            file:fileData
        };

        google.script.run
            .withSuccessHandler(function(result){
                btn.disabled=false;
                btn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-paper-plane"></i><span>Confirm & Submit</span>';

                if(result&&result.success){
                    ANON_closeConfirmation();
                    ANON_close();

                    const form=document.getElementById("anonymousComplaintForm");
                    if(form)form.reset();
                    ANON_removeFile();
                    ANON_toggleOther(false);

                    document.getElementById("generatedId").innerText=result.complaintId;

                    const emailNotice=document.getElementById("emailSendNotice");
                    if(emailNotice){
                        if(result.emailSent){
                            emailNotice.className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3";
                            emailNotice.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check mr-1"></i> Anonymous Complaint-এর notification email পাঠানো হয়েছে।';
                        }else{
                            emailNotice.className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3";
                            emailNotice.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation mr-1"></i> Complaint save হয়েছে, কিন্তু notification email পাঠানো যায়নি।';
                        }
                        emailNotice.classList.remove("hidden");
                    }

                    // Show the existing success card, without changing the existing complaint flow.
                    const complaintForm=document.getElementById("complaintForm");
                    const successCard=document.getElementById("successCard");
                    if(complaintForm)complaintForm.classList.add("hidden");
                    if(successCard)successCard.classList.remove("hidden");
                }else{
                    alert("ত্রুটি: "+(result?(result.message||result.error||"সফলভাবে জমা হয়নি"):"সফলভাবে জমা হয়নি"));
                }
            })
            .withFailureHandler(function(err){
                btn.disabled=false;
                btn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-paper-plane"></i><span>Confirm & Submit</span>';
                alert("সার্ভার ত্রুটি: "+(err&&err.message?err.message:"Unknown error"));
            })
            .submitAnonymousComplaint(record);

    }catch(err){
        btn.disabled=false;
        btn.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-paper-plane"></i><span>Confirm & Submit</span>';
        alert("ফাইল প্রস্তুত করতে সমস্যা হয়েছে: "+err.message);
    }
};

document.addEventListener("keydown",function(e){
    if(e.key==="Escape"){
        const o=document.getElementById("anonymousComplaintOverlay");
        if(o&&!o.classList.contains("hidden"))ANON_close();
    }
});

document.addEventListener("keydown",function(e){if(e.key==="Escape"){const o=document.getElementById("contactUsOverlay");if(o&&o.classList.contains("show"))CONTACT_close()}})
})();
</script>

<section id="organogramOverlay" aria-hidden="true" onclick="OG_backdrop(event)">
  <div class="og-shell" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="ogTitle">
    <div class="og-head">
      <div class="og-head-row">
        <div class="og-title-wrap">
          <div class="og-kicker">Organization Structure</div>
          <div id="ogTitle" class="og-title">Our Organogram</div>
          <div class="og-subtitle">Reporting flow অনুযায়ী Management → Team → Employee hierarchy</div>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="og-close" onclick="OG_close()" aria-label="Back to main portal"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left"></i><span>মূল পোর্টালে ফিরুন</span></button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="og-toolbar">
      <div class="og-search-wrap"><i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass"></i><input id="ogSearch" class="og-search" type="search" placeholder="Name, designation বা team দিয়ে খুঁজুন..." oninput="OG_render()"></div>
      <select id="ogDivision" class="og-select" onchange="OG_render()"><option value="">সব Division</option></select>
      <div class="og-stat"><i class="fa-solid fa-users"></i> People: <b id="ogPeople">0</b></div>
      <div class="og-stat"><i class="fa-solid fa-people-group"></i> Teams: <b id="ogTeams">0</b></div>
      <div class="og-stat"><i class="fa-solid fa-layer-group"></i> Levels: <b id="ogLevels">0</b></div>
      <div class="og-zoom" aria-label="Organogram zoom controls">
        <button type="button" onclick="OG_zoomOut()" title="Zoom out" aria-label="Zoom out"><i class="fa-solid fa-minus"></i></button>

        <span id="ogZoomLabel">80%</span>
        <button type="button" onclick="OG_zoomIn()" title="Zoom in" aria-label="Zoom in"><i class="fa-solid fa-plus"></i></button>
      </div>

    <div id="ogBody" class="og-body">

    </div>

  <div class="og-team-pop-head"><span id="ogTeamPopTitle">Teams</span><button type="button" class="og-team-close" onclick="OG_closeTeams()"><i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i></button></div>

<script id="organogram-addon-js">
(function(){
  "use strict";
  let OG_rows=[];
  let OG_loaded=false;
  let OG_scale=0.80;

  function OG_norm(v){return String(v==null?"":v).replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g,"").replace(/\u00A0/g," ").trim().replace(/\s+/g," ").toLowerCase();}
  function OG_escape(v){return String(v==null?"":v).replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;").replace(/>/g,"&gt;").replace(/"/g,"&quot;").replace(/'/g,"&#039;");}
  function OG_initial(v){let s=String(v||"?").trim();if(!s)return"?";let p=s.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);return (p.length>1?(p[0].charAt(0)+p[p.length-1].charAt(0)):p[0].charAt(0)).toUpperCase();}
  function OG_photo(v){let u=String(v||"").trim();if(!u)return"";let m=u.match(/\/d\/([A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,})/);if(!m)m=u.match(/[?&]id=([A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,})/);if(m&&m[1])return"https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id="+encodeURIComponent(m[1])+"&sz=w400-h400";return u;}

  window.OG_open=function(){
    const o=document.getElementById("organogramOverlay");if(!o)return;

    // Organogram is a full-page view. Cleanly close other full-page views first.
    try{ if(window.JOB_exitFullView) window.JOB_exitFullView(); }catch(e){}
    try{ if(window.NBOARD_exitFullView) window.NBOARD_exitFullView(); }catch(e){}

    const header=document.getElementById("topHeaderBlock");
    if(header){
      const rect=header.getBoundingClientRect();
      const h=Math.max(60,Math.round(rect.height||72));
      const w=Math.max(0,Math.round(rect.width||window.innerWidth));
      const cs=window.getComputedStyle(header);
      const padLeft=parseFloat(cs.paddingLeft)||0;
      const padRight=parseFloat(cs.paddingRight)||0;
      const innerPad=Math.max(10,Math.round(Math.max(padLeft,padRight)));
      document.body.style.setProperty("--og-header-height",h+"px");
      document.body.style.setProperty("--og-layout-width",w+"px");
      document.body.style.setProperty("--og-inner-pad",innerPad+"px");
    }

    const main=document.querySelector("main");
    const jobCard=document.getElementById("jobOpportunityCard");
    const jobView=document.getElementById("jobOpportunityFullView");
    const noticeView=document.getElementById("noticeBoardFullView");
    if(main) main.style.display="none";
    if(jobCard) jobCard.style.display="none";
    if(jobView) jobView.style.display="none";
    if(noticeView) noticeView.style.display="none";

    document.body.classList.remove("dashboard-mode","notice-board-mode","job-opportunity-mode");
    document.body.classList.add("organogram-mode");
    o.setAttribute("aria-hidden","false");
    OG_closeTeams();
    if(!OG_loaded)OG_load(); else OG_render();
    window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:"auto"});
  };

  window.OG_close=function(){
    const o=document.getElementById("organogramOverlay");if(!o)return;
    document.body.classList.remove("organogram-mode");
    o.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");
    OG_closeTeams();

    // Restore the normal portal so every existing navigation button works again.
    const main=document.querySelector("main");
    const jobCard=document.getElementById("jobOpportunityCard");
    const jobView=document.getElementById("jobOpportunityFullView");
    const noticeView=document.getElementById("noticeBoardFullView");
    const topHeader=document.getElementById("topHeaderBlock");
    const tabNav=document.getElementById("tabNavContainer");
    if(main) main.style.display="";
    if(jobCard) jobCard.style.display="";
    if(jobView) jobView.style.display="none";
    if(noticeView) noticeView.style.display="none";
    if(topHeader) topHeader.classList.remove("hidden");
    if(tabNav) tabNav.classList.remove("hidden");
    const btnSubmit=document.getElementById("btnSubmitTab");
    const btnTrack=document.getElementById("btnTrackTab");
    if(btnSubmit) btnSubmit.classList.remove("hidden");
    if(btnTrack) btnTrack.classList.remove("hidden");

    document.body.classList.remove("notice-board-mode","job-opportunity-mode","dashboard-mode");
    document.body.style.removeProperty("--og-header-height");
    document.body.style.removeProperty("height");
    document.body.style.removeProperty("overflow");
    document.body.style.removeProperty("overflow-x");
    window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:"auto"});
  };
  // Keep the existing Notice / User Login navigation reachable while Organogram is open.
  (function OG_keepPortalNavLive(){
    function bind(){
      const notice=document.getElementById("noticeBoardNavBtn");
      const login=document.querySelector('#topHeaderBlock button[title="Dashboard Entrance"]');
      [notice,login].forEach(function(btn){
        if(!btn)return;
        btn.style.pointerEvents="auto";
        btn.style.position="relative";
        btn.style.zIndex="100002";
      });
    }
    if(document.readyState==="loading") document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",bind,{once:true}); else bind();
    window.setTimeout(bind,0);
  })();

  /* Organogram-only navigation bridge: close this view first, then let the
     existing Notice / User Login inline handlers execute unchanged. */
  (function OG_navigationBridge(){
    document.addEventListener("click",function(e){
      if(!document.body.classList.contains("organogram-mode")) return;
      const notice=e.target.closest && e.target.closest("#noticeBoardNavBtn");
      const login=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#topHeaderBlock button[title="Dashboard Entrance"]');
      if(!notice && !login) return;
      if(typeof window.OG_close === "function") window.OG_close();
    },true);
  })();

  window.OG_backdrop=function(e){return;};
  window.OG_closeTeams=function(){const p=document.getElementById("ogTeamPop");if(p){p.classList.remove("show");p.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");}};

  function OG_setZoom(v){
    OG_scale=Math.max(0.45,Math.min(1.15,Number(v)||0.80));
    const forest=document.querySelector('#ogCanvas .og-forest');
    if(forest){forest.style.transform='scale('+OG_scale+')';forest.style.marginBottom=(forest.scrollHeight*(1-OG_scale))+'px';}
    const label=document.getElementById('ogZoomLabel');if(label)label.textContent=Math.round(OG_scale*100)+'%';
  }
  window.OG_zoomIn=function(){OG_setZoom(OG_scale+0.10);};
  window.OG_zoomOut=function(){OG_setZoom(OG_scale-0.10);};
  window.OG_fit=function(){
    const body=document.getElementById('ogBody'),forest=document.querySelector('#ogCanvas .og-forest');
    if(!body||!forest)return;
    const natural=Math.max(1,forest.scrollWidth);
    const available=Math.max(280,body.clientWidth-32);
    const ratio=available/natural;
    /* Prefer readable cards. If the full tree cannot fit, allow horizontal scroll
       rather than reducing the entire chart to 45%. */
    const minReadable=window.innerWidth<=560?0.58:(window.innerWidth<=800?0.62:0.68);
    const target=Math.min(1,Math.max(minReadable,ratio));
    OG_setZoom(target);
    const b=document.getElementById('ogBody');
    if(b) b.scrollLeft=0;
  };

  window.OG_load=function(){
    const c=document.getElementById("ogCanvas");if(c)c.innerHTML='<div class="og-loading"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i><div>Organogram loading...</div></div>';
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      if(!res||!res.success){OG_empty(res&&res.message?res.message:"Organogram data load করা যায়নি।");return;}
      OG_rows=Array.isArray(res.rows)?res.rows:[];OG_loaded=true;OG_fillDivisions(res.divisions||[]);OG_render();
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err){OG_empty("Organogram data load করতে সমস্যা হয়েছে।");console.error(err);}).getOrganogramData();
  };

  function OG_fillDivisions(ds){
    const s=document.getElementById("ogDivision");if(!s)return;
    const current=String(s.value||"");s.innerHTML='<option value="">সব Division</option>';
    (ds||[]).forEach(function(d){const o=document.createElement("option");o.value=d;o.textContent=d;s.appendChild(o);});
    if((ds||[]).some(function(d){return OG_norm(d)===OG_norm(current)}))s.value=current;
  }

  function OG_buildPeople(){
    const selected=String((document.getElementById("ogDivision")||{}).value||"").trim();
    const selectedKey=OG_norm(selected);
    const rows=OG_rows.filter(function(r){return !selectedKey||OG_norm(r.division)===selectedKey;});
    const map={};
    rows.forEach(function(r){
      const name=String(r.name||"").trim();if(!name)return;
      const key=OG_norm(name);
      if(!map[key])map[key]={key:key,name:name,designation:String(r.designation||"").trim(),photo:String(r.photo||"").trim(),divisions:[],teams:[],reportsTo:String(r.reportsTo||"").trim()};
      const p=map[key];
      if(!p.designation&&r.designation)p.designation=String(r.designation).trim();
      if(!p.photo&&r.photo)p.photo=String(r.photo).trim();
      const div=String(r.division||"").trim(),team=String(r.team||"").trim();
      if(div&&!p.divisions.some(function(x){return OG_norm(x)===OG_norm(div)}))p.divisions.push(div);
      if(team&&!p.teams.some(function(x){return OG_norm(x)===OG_norm(team)}))p.teams.push(team);
      if(!p.reportsTo&&r.reportsTo)p.reportsTo=String(r.reportsTo).trim();
    });
    return {map:map,rows:rows};
  }

  window.OG_render=function(){
    const canvas=document.getElementById("ogCanvas");if(!canvas)return;
    const built=OG_buildPeople(),map=built.map;
    const search=OG_norm((document.getElementById("ogSearch")||{}).value||"");
    const people=Object.keys(map).map(function(k){return map[k];});
    const teamSet={};people.forEach(function(p){p.teams.forEach(function(t){teamSet[OG_norm(t)]=t;});});
    document.getElementById("ogPeople").textContent=people.length;
    document.getElementById("ogTeams").textContent=Object.keys(teamSet).length;

    if(!people.length){document.getElementById("ogLevels").textContent="0";OG_empty("এই Division-এ কোনো Organogram data পাওয়া যায়নি।");return;}

    const visible=search?people.filter(function(p){return OG_norm(p.name).includes(search)||OG_norm(p.designation).includes(search)||p.teams.some(function(t){return OG_norm(t).includes(search);});}):people;
    if(!visible.length){document.getElementById("ogLevels").textContent="0";OG_empty("আপনার search-এর সাথে কোনো profile মেলেনি।");return;}

    const visibleMap={};visible.forEach(function(p){visibleMap[p.key]=p;});
    const children={};visible.forEach(function(p){children[p.key]=[];});
    visible.forEach(function(p){const parentKey=OG_norm(p.reportsTo);if(parentKey&&visibleMap[parentKey]&&parentKey!==p.key)children[parentKey].push(p.key);});

    const hasParent={};visible.forEach(function(p){hasParent[p.key]=!!(p.reportsTo&&visibleMap[OG_norm(p.reportsTo)]&&OG_norm(p.reportsTo)!==p.key);});
    const roots=visible.filter(function(p){return !hasParent[p.key];});

    // If a person reports to someone outside the selected Division, keep them as a root instead of losing the data.
    const depthMemo={};
    function depth(key,stack){if(depthMemo[key]!=null)return depthMemo[key];stack=stack||{};if(stack[key])return 0;stack[key]=true;const p=visibleMap[key];const pk=p&&OG_norm(p.reportsTo);if(!pk||!visibleMap[pk]||pk===key){depthMemo[key]=0;return 0;}depthMemo[key]=depth(pk,stack)+1;return depthMemo[key];}
    let maxDepth=0;visible.forEach(function(p){maxDepth=Math.max(maxDepth,depth(p.key));});
    document.getElementById("ogLevels").textContent=String(maxDepth+1);

    const matched={};if(search)visible.forEach(function(p){if(OG_norm(p.name).includes(search)||OG_norm(p.designation).includes(search)||p.teams.some(function(t){return OG_norm(t).includes(search);}))matched[p.key]=true;});

    const forest=document.createElement("div");forest.className="og-forest";
    const safeRoots=roots.length?roots:visible.slice(0,1);
    safeRoots.forEach(function(root){forest.appendChild(OG_tree(root.key,visibleMap,children,matched));});
    canvas.innerHTML="";canvas.appendChild(forest);
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      const isSmall=window.innerWidth<=900;
      OG_setZoom(isSmall?0.62:0.80);
      OG_fit();
    });
    const legend=document.createElement("div");legend.className="og-legend";legend.innerHTML='<span><i class="og-dot"></i> Reporting connection</span><span><i class="og-dot warn"></i> Reporting manager outside selected Division</span><span><i class="og-dot dash"></i> Supporting/root profile</span>';canvas.appendChild(legend);
  };

  function OG_tree(rootKey,map,children,matched){
    const wrap=document.createElement("div");wrap.className="og-tree";
    function makeNode(key){
      const p=map[key],node=document.createElement("div");node.className="og-node-wrap";
      const card=document.createElement("article");card.className="og-card"+(matched[key]?" og-match":"")+(!p.reportsTo?" og-no-parent":"")+((p.reportsTo&&!map[OG_norm(p.reportsTo)])?" og-support":"");
      const photo=OG_photo(p.photo);const av=document.createElement("div");av.className="og-avatar";
      if(photo){av.innerHTML='<img src="'+OG_escape(photo)+'" alt="'+OG_escape(p.name)+'" loading="lazy" onerror="this.style.display=\'none\';this.parentElement.textContent=\''+OG_escape(OG_initial(p.name))+ '\';">';}else av.textContent=OG_initial(p.name);
      const name=document.createElement("div");name.className="og-name";name.title=p.name;name.textContent=p.name;
      const des=document.createElement("div");des.className="og-des";des.textContent=p.designation||"";
      const meta=document.createElement("div");meta.className="og-meta";
      if(p.teams.length){const tb=document.createElement("button");tb.type="button";tb.className="og-pill og-pill-team";tb.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-users"></i> '+p.teams.length+' Team'+(p.teams.length>1?'s':'');tb.onclick=function(e){e.stopPropagation();OG_showTeams(p,e.currentTarget);};meta.appendChild(tb);}
      if(p.divisions.length){const db=document.createElement("span");db.className="og-pill og-pill-div";db.innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-building"></i> '+OG_escape(p.divisions.length>1?p.divisions.length+' Divisions':p.divisions[0]);meta.appendChild(db);}
      card.appendChild(av);card.appendChild(name);card.appendChild(des);card.appendChild(meta);
      if(p.reportsTo){const rep=document.createElement("div");rep.className="og-report";rep.textContent="Reports to: "+p.reportsTo;card.appendChild(rep);}
      node.appendChild(card);
      return node;
    }
    function build(key){
      const node=makeNode(key),kids=(children[key]||[]).slice();
      if(kids.length){
        const branch=document.createElement("div");branch.className="og-branch";node.appendChild(branch);
        const ch=document.createElement("div");ch.className="og-children";ch.style.setProperty("--line-left",kids.length===1?"50%":"calc(50% / "+kids.length+")");ch.style.setProperty("--line-right",kids.length===1?"50%":"calc(50% / "+kids.length+")");
        kids.forEach(function(k){const child=document.createElement("div");child.className="og-child";child.appendChild(build(k));ch.appendChild(child);});
        node.appendChild(ch);
      }
      return node;
    }
    wrap.appendChild(build(rootKey));return wrap;
  }

  function OG_showTeams(p,anchor){
    const pop=document.getElementById("ogTeamPop"),list=document.getElementById("ogTeamPopList"),title=document.getElementById("ogTeamPopTitle");if(!pop||!list)return;
    title.textContent=p.name+" • "+p.teams.length+" Team"+(p.teams.length>1?'s':'');list.innerHTML=p.teams.map(function(t){return '<span class="og-team-item">'+OG_escape(t)+'</span>';}).join("");
    pop.classList.add("show");pop.setAttribute("aria-hidden","false");
    const r=anchor.getBoundingClientRect(),w=330;let left=Math.min(Math.max(14,r.left),window.innerWidth-w-14),top=r.bottom+9;if(top+250>window.innerHeight)top=Math.max(14,r.top-250);pop.style.left=left+"px";pop.style.top=top+"px";
  }
  function OG_empty(msg){const c=document.getElementById("ogCanvas");if(c)c.innerHTML='<div class="og-empty"><i class="fa-solid fa-sitemap" style="font-size:30px;color:#94a3b8;margin-bottom:10px"></i><strong>Organogram</strong><span>'+OG_escape(msg)+'</span></div>';}
  document.addEventListener("keydown",function(e){if(e.key==="Escape"){const o=document.getElementById("organogramOverlay");if(o&&document.body.classList.contains("organogram-mode")){if(document.getElementById("ogTeamPop")&&document.getElementById("ogTeamPop").classList.contains("show"))OG_closeTeams();else OG_close();}}});
  document.addEventListener("click",function(e){const p=document.getElementById("ogTeamPop");if(p&&p.classList.contains("show")&&!p.contains(e.target)&&!e.target.closest(".og-pill-team"))OG_closeTeams();});
  window.addEventListener("resize",function(){if(document.body.classList.contains("organogram-mode")){clearTimeout(window.__ogResizeTimer);window.__ogResizeTimer=setTimeout(OG_fit,120);}});
})();
</script>


<script id="FULL_PAGE_BILINGUAL_PATCH">
(function () {
  'use strict';

  const BN_TO_EN = {"নোটিশ":"Notice","Organogram":"Organogram","বাংলা":"Bangla","English":"English","ইউজার লগইন":"User Login","⚡ একসাথে গড়ি আরো উন্নত সেবা":"⚡ Building Better Services Together","🎯   // Reversible full-page translation state. Each DOM text node remembers
  // which language it currently contains, so BN → EN → BN works reliably.
  const textState = new WeakMap();
  const attrState = new WeakMap();
  const sortedBN = Object.keys(BN_TO_EN).sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length);
  const sortedEN = Object.keys(EN_TO_BN).sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length);

  function clean(v) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); }

  function translateString(value, from, to) {
    const raw = String(value == null ? '' : value);
    if (!raw || from === to) return raw;
    const map = from === 'bn' && to === 'en' ? BN_TO_EN : EN_TO_BN;
    const keys = from === 'bn' && to === 'en' ? sortedBN : sortedEN;
    const exact = clean(raw);
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(map, exact)) {
      const lead = raw.match(/^\s*/)?.[0] || '';
      const trail = raw.match(/\s*$/)?.[0] || '';
      return lead + map[exact] + trail;
    }
    let out = raw;
    keys.forEach(function(key) { if (key) out = out.split(key).join(map[key]); });
    return out;
  }

  function pageLang() {
    try { return (typeof currentLang === 'string' && currentLang === 'en') ? 'en' : 'bn'; }
    catch (e) { return 'bn'; }
  }

  function skip(node) {
    const p = node && (node.parentElement || node);
    if (!p) return true;
    if (p.closest && p.closest('[data-no-i18n="true"]')) return true;
    return /^(SCRIPT|STYLE|NOSCRIPT|CODE|PRE)$/.test(p.tagName);
  }

  function translateTextNode(node, target) {
    if (!node || !node.nodeValue || skip(node)) return;
    const current = node.nodeValue;
    let st = textState.get(node);
    if (!st) {
      const c = clean(current);
      st = { lang: Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(EN_TO_BN, c) ? 'en' : 'bn', value: current };
      textState.set(node, st);
    } else if (st.value !== current) {
      // Dynamic module re-render: the new text is already in the active page language.
      st.value = current;
      st.lang = target;
    }
    if (st.lang === target) return;
    const next = translateString(st.value, st.lang, target);
    if (next !== st.value) node.nodeValue = next;
    st.value = next;
    st.lang = target;
  }

  function translateAttributes(root, target) {
    const els = root.querySelectorAll ? root.querySelectorAll('[placeholder],[title],[aria-label],[alt]') : [];
    els.forEach(function(el) {
      if (el.closest('[data-no-i18n="true"]')) return;
      let states = attrState.get(el);
      if (!states) { states = {}; attrState.set(el, states); }
      ['placeholder','title','aria-label','alt'].forEach(function(attr) {
        if (!el.hasAttribute(attr)) return;
        const current = el.getAttribute(attr);
        let st = states[attr];
        if (!st) {
          const c = clean(current);
          st = states[attr] = { lang: Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(EN_TO_BN, c) ? 'en' : 'bn', value: current };
        } else if (st.value !== current) {
          st.value = current;
          st.lang = target;
        }
        if (st.lang !== target) {
          const next = translateString(st.value, st.lang, target);
          if (next !== st.value) el.setAttribute(attr, next);
          st.value = next;
          st.lang = target;
        }
      });
    });
  }

  function translatePage(target) {
    if (!document.body) return;
    const walker = document.createTreeWalker(document.body, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
    const nodes = [];
    let n;
    while ((n = walker.nextNode())) nodes.push(n);
    nodes.forEach(function(node) { translateTextNode(node, target); });
    translateAttributes(document.body, target);
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('lang', target === 'en' ? 'en' : 'bn');
  }

  const originalSetLanguage = window.setLanguage;
  if (typeof originalSetLanguage === 'function' && !window.__fullPageLanguageWrappedV3) {
    window.setLanguage = function(lang) {
      const target = lang === 'en' ? 'en' : 'bn';
      const result = originalSetLanguage.apply(this, arguments);
      [0,80,200,500,1000].forEach(function(delay) {
        setTimeout(function() { translatePage(target); }, delay);
      });
      return result;
    };
    window.__fullPageLanguageWrappedV3 = true;
  }

  if (!window.__fullPageLanguageObserverV3) {
    const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
      let added = false;
      mutations.forEach(function(m) {
        if (m.type === 'childList' && m.addedNodes && m.addedNodes.length) added = true;
      });
      if (!added) return;
      clearTimeout(window.__fullPageLanguageTimerV3);
      window.__fullPageLanguageTimerV3 = setTimeout(function() { translatePage(pageLang()); }, 40);
    });
    observer.observe(document.body, {subtree:true, childList:true});
    window.__fullPageLanguageObserverV3 = observer;
  }

  function boot() { translatePage(pageLang()); }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot, {once:true});
  else boot();

  window.translateEntirePage = function(lang) { translatePage(lang === 'en' ? 'en' : 'bn'); };
})();
</script>

</body>
</html>
